| 'complete' | 'blocked' | 'decide'\n\nexport interface IterationStats {\n  iteration: number\n  durationMs: number\n}\n\nexport interface RocketLoopAppProps {\n  tasks: Task[]\n  backend: AgentBackend\n  backendName: string\n  projectName: string\n  maxIterations: number\n  agentDir: string\n}\n\nexport function RocketLoopApp({\n  tasks,\n  backend,\n  backendName,\n  projectName,\n  maxIterations,\n  agentDir,\n}: RocketLoopAppProps) {\n  const [selectedTask, setSelectedTask] = useState<Task | null>(null)\n  const { state: loopState, start } = useLoopRunner()\n\n  const phase: AppState =\n    selectedTask === null\n      ? 'selecting'\n      : loopState.phase === 'idle' || loopState.phase === 'running'\n        ? 'running'\n        : loopState.phase === 'max-reached'\n          ? 'complete'\n          : (loopState.phase as AppState)\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (selectedTask && loopState.phase === 'idle') {\n      start({\n        task: selectedTask,\n        backend,\n        maxIterations,\n        agentDir,\n      })\n    }\n  }, [selectedTask, loopState.phase, start, backend, maxIterations, agentDir])\n\n  function handleTaskSelect(task: Task | null) {\n    setSelectedTask(task)\n  }\n\n  function handleDecideAnswer(_answer: string) {\n    // After a decision, restart the loop\n    if (selectedTask) {\n      start({\n        task: selectedTask,\n        backend,\n        maxIterations,\n        agentDir,\n      })\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (phase === 'selecting') {\n    return (\n      <TaskSelector\n        tasks={tasks}\n        backendName={backendName}\n        projectName={projectName}\n        onSelect={handleTaskSelect}\n      />\n    )\n  }\n\n  if (phase === 'running') {\n    const taskId = selectedTask?.id ?? 0\n    return (\n      <Box flexDirection=\"column\">\n        <IterationHeader n={loopState.currentIteration} max={maxIterations} taskId={taskId} />\n        <SpinnerPreview lines={loopState.outputLines} />\n      </Box>\n    )\n  }\n\n  if (phase === 'complete') {\n    const outcome = loopState.phase === 'max-reached' ? 'max-iterations' : 'complete'\n    return (\n      <CompletionReport\n        outcome={outcome}\n        task={selectedTask}\n        iterations={loopState.iterations}\n        totalMs={loopState.totalMs}\n        iterationStats={loopState.iterationStats}\n        summary=\"\"\n      />\n    )\n  }\n\n  if (phase === 'blocked') {\n    return <BlockedScreen reason={loopState.blockedReason ?? 'Unknown reason'} />\n  }\n\n  // phase === 'decide'\n  return (\n    <DecideScreen\n      question={loopState.decideQuestion ?? 'No question provided'}\n      agentDir={agentDir}\n      onDecide={handleDecideAnswer}\n    />\n  )\n}\n\nexport { RocketLoopApp as default }\n"}}

0,\n  outputLines: [] as string[],\n  iterations: 0,\n  totalMs: 0,\n  iterationStats: [],\n}\n\n// Mock the loop runner hook so we don't actually run the loop\nvi.mock('./hooks/useLoopRunner.js', () => ({\n  useLoopRunner: () => ({\n    state: mockLoopState,\n    start: mockStart,\n    stop: mockStop,\n    togglePause: mockTogglePause,\n    skip: mockSkip,\n    paused: mockPaused,\n  }),\n}))\n\nfunction makeTasks(): Task[] {\n  return [\n    {\n      id: 10,\n      title: 'Setup config',\n      description: 'desc',\n      category: 'config',\n      passes: false,\n      passCondition: 'config works',\n    },\n    {\n      id: 11,\n      title: 'Build feature',\n      description: 'desc',\n      category: 'functional',\n      passes: false,\n      passCondition: 'feature works',\n    },\n    {\n      id: 12,\n      title: 'Done task',\n      description: 'desc',\n      category: 'functional',\n      passes: true,\n      passCondition: 'done',\n    },\n  ]\n}\n\nfunction makeBackend(): AgentBackend {\n  return { name: 'copilot', spawn: vi.fn(), parseOutput: vi.fn() }\n}\n\nconst defaultProps = {\n  tasks: makeTasks(),\n  backend: makeBackend(),\n  backendName: 'copilot',\n  projectName: 'test-project',\n  maxIterations: 5,\n  agentDir: '/tmp/agent',\n}\n\nbeforeEach(() => {\n  vi.clearAllMocks()\n  mockPaused = false\n  mockLoopState = {\n task')\n  })\n\n  it('shows Auto option in selecting state', () => {\n    const { lastFrame } = render(<RocketLoopApp {...defaultProps} />)\n    expect(lastFrame()).toContain('Auto')\n  })\n\n  it('exports AppState type and default export', async () => {\n    const mod = await import('./RocketLoopApp.js')\n    expect(mod.RocketLoopApp).toBeDefined()\n    expect(mod.default).toBe(mod.RocketLoopApp)\n  })\n\n  it('renders with all tasks complete', () => {\n    const allDone: Task[] = [\n      {\n        id: 1,\n        title: 'Only task',\n        description: 'desc',\n        category: 'functional',\n        passes: true,\n        passCondition: 'done',\n      },\n    ]\n    const { lastFrame } = render(<RocketLoopApp {...defaultProps} () => {\n  it('does not call stop/exit when q is pressed in selecting state', () => {\n    const { stdin } = render(<RocketLoopApp {...defaultProps} />)\n    stdin.write('q')\n
